## `AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** PMI-Metallography Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Boiler Alloy Metallographic Creep Degradation & Small-Tube Radiography
- **Intro:** PMI-Metallography Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Boiler Alloy Metallographic Creep Degradation & Small-Tube Radiography — Verify Work Permit and Radiation Safety Zone: Radiation dose rate at the boundary must be ≤ 2.5 µSv/h. Exclusion zone radius must be exactly 20 m ± 0.5 m.
- **Key tolerance:** Radiation dose rate at the boundary must be ≤ 2.5 µSv/h. Exclusion zone radius must be exactly 20 m ± 0.5 m.
- **Standards:** `ISO 17636-1:2022`, `ISO 643:2019`, `ASTM E112-13 Standard Test Methods for Determining Average Grain`, `ISO 9712:2021`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AGO-2513-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
